The distance from the target is estimated from pathloss measurements and is assumed to be noise-free. The proposed system consists of a receiver that is stationed at the origin and a mobile agent. The system does not rely on GPS, angle sensing, or multiple anchor nodes to localize the target. The proposed method first identifies the target quadrant and estimates its position. Consecutive target position estimates are then used to determine the motion vector and update the agent trajectory during pursuit. We show that a maximum of thirteen steps are required to localize the target and determine its motion vector. Simulation results show that the agent can successfully make a transition from search to pursuit phase, and also maintain a bounded tracking error.

</details>


### [242] [Geometric View on Integrated Cascaded Channel of IRS-Aided Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27972)
*Yunli Li,Young Jin Chun*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对混合智能反射面(IRS)系统，提出基于几何模型的部署策略和机会关联策略，突破了以往研究中仅适用于特定条件的局限性，显著提升了系统性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究在IRS关联策略中盲目假设IRS位于收发器之间且高度差远小于链路距离，这种简化假设虽便于分析但导致性能次优，无法获得全局最优的IRS部署策略。

Method: 建立集成路径损耗距离的几何模型（乘积距离路径损耗对应Cassini oval，和距离路径损耗对应Ellipse），据此确定混合IRS的最优位置，并设计新型机会关联策略。

Result: 仿真验证表明，所提方法显著优于传统最近关联策略，尤其在混合IRS和主动IRS场景下性能提升更为明显。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于几何模型的部署策略和机会关联策略能够实现混合IRS系统的全局最优部署，克服了现有方法的局限性，为IRS辅助无线网络的优化部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The hybrid intelligent reflecting surface (IRS) architecture is a novel technology that leverages the advantages of both passive and active IRS; the passive IRS offers a large aperture, while the active IRS provides additional power amplification. Prior studies have shown that the optimal performance of IRS-assisted wireless networks is achieved when the passive IRS is deployed near the transceivers and the active IRS is closer to the receiver with decreasing amplification power, assuming that the IRS is located between transceivers and that the height difference between IRS and transceivers is much smaller than the link distance between transceivers. However, most of the prior works on IRS blindly adopted this assumption in the IRS association policy, which essentially becomes a partial selection strategy that offers analytical simplicity at the cost of sub-optimal performance. This limitation motivated us to find the globally optimal deployment strategy for all types of IRS. To this end, we first employ the \emph{geometric models} for integrated path loss distance (known as Cassini oval and Ellipse for product- and sum-distance path loss laws, respectively) and use them to determine the optimal locations of the hybrid IRS. Then, we design a novel \emph{opportunistic association policy} for hybrid IRS based on the integrated path loss model. Furthermore, we validate our proposed methods through simulations and show that they significantly outperform the conventional nearest association policy, especially for hybrid and active IRS.

</details>


### [243] [Layered Architecture for Mobile Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28083)
*Qingwen Liu,Mingqing Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“移动AI堆栈”概念，针对动态移动环境下的AI系统，构建了一个包含移动能源网络、能效AI芯片、云-边-端基础设施、分布式AI模型和具身AI应用五个紧密耦合层的移动感知架构框架，并讨论了关键研究挑战与未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI框架主要基于静态的、以云为中心的计算范式，忽视了移动性约束。然而，自动驾驶车辆、无人机、机器人和可穿戴设备等新兴智能应用需要在高度动态和移动的环境中运行，这引入了移动性作为整个AI生态系统的基本约束，影响能源供应、计算和智能部署。

Method: 引入移动AI堆栈的概念，这是一个移动感知的架构框架，整合了五个紧密耦合的层次：移动能源网络、能效AI芯片、云-边-端基础设施、分布式AI模型和具身AI应用。该框架提供了系统性视角，探讨能源交付、计算架构、通信网络和AI算法如何协同演进。

Result: 提出了Mobile AI Stack框架，为理解大规模移动智能系统中能源交付、计算架构、通信网络和AI算法的协同演进提供了系统性视角，并识别了构建可扩展、可靠、能效高的移动AI系统的关键研究挑战。

Conclusion: Mobile AI Stack为下一代基础设施提供了概念蓝图，该基础设施深度融合了计算、能源和通信网络以支持移动智能，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly evolving from a centralized computing capability into a pervasive infrastructure that interacts directly with the physical world. While recent perspectives highlight the roles of energy, chips, infrastructure, models, and applications in enabling large-scale AI systems, these frameworks primarily assume a static, cloud-centric computing paradigm. However, emerging intelligent applications, including autonomous vehicles, drones, robots, and wearable systems, require AI to operate in highly dynamic and mobile environments. This shift introduces mobility as a fundamental constraint across the entire AI ecosystem, affecting energy supply, computation, and intelligence deployment. In this article, we introduce the concept of the Mobile AI Stack, a mobility-aware architectural framework that integrates five tightly coupled layers: mobile energy networks, energy-efficient AI chips, cloud-edge-mobile infrastructure, distributed AI models, and embodied AI applications. The proposed framework provides a systematic perspective for understanding how energy delivery, computing architectures, communication networks, and AI algorithms must co-evolve to support large-scale mobile intelligence. We further discuss key research challenges and future directions toward building scalable, reliable, and energy-efficient mobile AI systems. Mobile AI Stack offers a conceptual blueprint of the next-generation infrastructure which deeply integrates the networks of computation, energy, and communications for mobile intelligence.

</details>


### [244] [PCAP-LM: An LLM-Native Text Representation for TLS Bulk Traffic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28100)
*Xavier Marjou,Lucas Tamic,Ilan Jaffeux-Cheniout*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了PCAP-LM，一种面向LLM的网络流量文本表示方法，通过PacketGlyphs字母表和PMI-BPE分词器实现812倍压缩，使LLM在流量取证问答任务中达到99.3%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型(LLM)在网络流量分析中具有强大推理能力，但标准捕获格式及其文本表示过于冗长，超出LLM上下文窗口限制两个数量级，无法直接应用于完整流量分析。

Method: 提出PCAP-LM方法：1) 设计PacketGlyphs新型ASCII字母表，编码包方向、TCP/TLS状态、对数尺度大小和包间延迟；2) 采用约束PMI-BPE分词器和模式运行长度编码，激进压缩重复行为模式；3) 通过@REFS侧索引保留无损回溯原始数据包的能力。

Result: 在5G/4G TLS 1.3流量数据集上，BPE词汇表饱和于159个token，相比tshark -V实现812倍压缩，完整捕获可放入单个LLM上下文窗口。在30个保留文件的取证问答评估中，前沿LLM在PCAP-LM文档上达到99.3%准确率，而在token预算匹配的tshark -V前缀上仅51.0%。TCP重传存在24%假阴性率的已知盲点。

Conclusion: PCAP-LM成功将网络流量压缩至LLM可用规模并显著提升问答准确性，但设计中的有损特性引入已知盲点，扩展到异构混合协议环境需要重新训练词汇表。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer powerful reasoning capabilities for network traffic analysis, but standard capture formats and their textual equivalents are prohibitively verbose, overflowing LLM context windows by two orders of magnitude. We present PCAP-LM, a flow-centric, LLM-native text representation that acts as a lossy knowledge extraction step rather than a standard compression tool: raw captures are transcoded into semantic summaries using PacketGlyphs - a novel ASCII alphabet coined in this paper that encodes packet direction, TCP/TLS state, log-scale size, and inter-packet delay. Combined with a constrained PMI-BPE tokenizer and motif run-length encoding, repetitive behavioural patterns are aggressively collapsed. A @REFS side-index preserves lossless drill-down into the original packets. Evaluated on a homogeneous corpus of 5G/4G TLS 1.3 bulk-download traffic, the BPE vocabulary fully saturates at 159 tokens, achieving an 812x size reduction over tshark -V and fitting entire captures within a single LLM context window. In a forensic question-answering evaluation over 30 held-out files, a frontier LLM achieves 99.3% accuracy from PCAP-LM documents versus 51.0% from a token-budget-matched tshark -V prefix. The lossy design introduces known blind spots - most notably a 24% false-negative rate for TCP retransmissions - and extending to heterogeneous mixed-protocol environments will require vocabulary retraining.

</details>


### [245] [Powering Net-Zero 6G: Packetized Energy Management for Grid-Interactive Telecom Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28111)
*Adnan Aijaz,Xinyi Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出数据包化能源管理（PEM）框架，将6G基础设施转化为能源感知和电网交互资产，通过将灵活需求表示为可调度的能源数据包来优化能源使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向净零6G网络的转型需要超越传统RAN效率机制的能源管理方法。随着未来网络集成AI原生操作、边缘智能、密集部署、可再生能源和存储，RAN将成为不断增长的电力消耗者和分布式能源灵活性的来源，因此需要新的能源管理框架。

Method: 提出数据包化能源管理（PEM）框架，将灵活需求表示为可调度的能源数据包，可根据本地约束、可再生能源可用性、碳强度、价格和通信优先级进行准入、延迟或重塑。同时设计了PEM使能的基站模型、PEM集成的RAN架构，以及电信虚拟电厂（VPP）概念用于聚合PEM使能站点。

Result: 仿真结果证明了PEM在峰值感知运行、改善可再生能源利用和停电韧性服务连续性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: 本文展示了PEM框架能够将6G基础设施转化为能源感知、电网交互资产，并讨论了电信-能源协同设计的开放挑战。

Abstract: The transition to net-zero 6G requires energy-management approaches that go beyond conventional RAN efficiency mechanisms. As future networks integrate AI-native operation, edge intelligence, dense deployments, renewables, and storage, the RAN will become both a growing power consumer and a source of distributed energy flexibility. This paper introduces packetized energy management (PEM) as a framework for transforming 6G infrastructure into energy-aware, grid-interactive assets. PEM represents flexible demand as schedulable energy packets that can be admitted, deferred, or reshaped according to local constraints, renewable availability, carbon intensity, price, and communication priorities. We present a PEM-enabled base-station model, a RAN architecture for PEM integration, and the telecoms virtual power plant (VPP) concept for aggregating PEM-enabled sites. Simulation results demonstrate PEM's potential for peak-aware operation, improved renewable utilization, and outage-resilient service continuity. The paper also discusses open challenges for telco-energy co-design.

</details>


### [246] [Coexistence of 5G NR and Wi Fi 6E/7 at 6 GHz: Experimental Interference Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28213)
*Rafik Zitouni,Demos Serghiou,Ali Dagdeviren,Tajinder Randhawa,Edwards Udean,Hanli Dong,Riccardo Pozza,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文首次测量了商用VLP Wi-Fi 6E/7设备对运行中的5G NR系统（6 GHz频段）的传导干扰，结果表明在-75 dBm以下无性能损失，且合规VLP设备会在造成干扰前通过LBT机制退出信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着6 GHz频段被开放用于Wi-Fi 6E/7等免授权设备，这些低功率设备与已部署的5G NR系统之间的共存问题需要实验验证。本文旨在通过实际测量确定VLP Wi-Fi设备对5G NR基站上行和终端下行接收链路的干扰阈值。

Method: 研究者构建了基于OpenAirInterface的软件无线电(SDR)测试平台，支持5G NR n102频段（6 GHz，40 MHz带宽，30 kHz子载波间隔）和完整的O-RAN/5G核心网。通过向5G系统注入可变功率的Wi-Fi信号，测量了gNB上行和UE下行路径的吞吐量、误块率和信噪比变化。并辅以链路预算分析将测量阈值映射到等效的VLP到受害设备距离。

Result: 实验表明：在注入功率低于-75 dBm时，5G接收机无性能损失；超过此阈值后性能逐渐恶化。UE在低数据速率下表现出更强抗干扰能力，且不受仅发送信标帧传输影响。链路预算分析显示等效距离完全落在545-685 MHz LBT排斥区域内。

Conclusion: 研究证实，符合规范的VLP Wi-Fi设备在5G NR接收机遭受干扰前会通过LBT机制退出信道，验证了VLP设备与5G NR系统的安全共存能力，为6 GHz频段共享使用提供了实验依据。

Abstract: This paper presents the first conducted-interference measurements of a commercial Very Low Power (VLP) Wi-Fi 6E/7 device into both the gNB uplink and UE downlink receiver chains of a live 5G New Radio (NR) system, using a complete O-RAN/SDR stack with 5G core in band~n102 (6\,GHz). We use a Software-Defined Radio (SDR) testbed built on OpenAirInterface with band~n102 support (40 MHz, 30 kHz Subcarrier Spacing). We sweep the injected Wi-Fi power and record throughput, block error rate, and signal-to-noise ratio on both the gNB uplink and UE downlink paths. Neither receiver shows measurable degradation below 75 dBm. Above this threshold, performance degrades progressively. The UE is more resilient at lower data rates and unaffected by beacon-only transmissions. A complementary link-budget analysis maps these measured thresholds to equivalent VLP-to-victim distances. These distances fall well inside the 545--685 Listen Before Talk (LBT) exclusion zone, confirming that a compliant VLP device would vacate the channel before its emissions could harm either receiver.

</details>


### [247] [Observing the Relationship between QoS Unpredictability, Prediction Error, and User Activity in a Remote Desktop Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28216)
*Keisuke Ishibashi,Xuliang Deng,Yoshiaki Kitaguchi,Kenichi Nagami,Ichiro Mizukoshi,Akira Sato,Daiyu Nobori*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析真实世界的远程桌面服务使用日志，发现RTT的平均值、时间波动及瞬时偏差均与用户活动显著相关，揭示了QoS不可预测性对用户行为的影响机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于远程桌面服务QoS与用户活动关系的研究多在实验环境下进行，样本量有限且无法反映真实使用场景，导致QoS时间波动与用户活动的关系未被充分探索。

Method: 利用真实世界的Thin-Telework System使用日志，分析用户层面的往返时间（RTT）、发送数据包数量和接收字节数的时间序列数据，研究QoS统计特征与用户活动的关联。

Result: 发现RTT的平均值、时间波动（如标准差）以及瞬时偏差均与用户活动显著相关，这些特征分别对应用户视角的QoS不可预测性和预测误差。

Conclusion: QoS的波动特征可能反映影响用户行为的心理机制，为理解和改善远程桌面服务的用户体验提供了新视角。

Abstract: With the increasing need for remote work, especially since the COVID-19 era, Remote Desktop Services (RDS) have become widely used. Because interactive RDS usage depends heavily on communication quality, some studies have investigated the relationship between QoS metrics and user activity in RDS. However, these works have een conducted in experimental environments, where the number of samples is limited and may not reflect real-world usage. Consequently, the relationship between temporal fluctuations in QoS and user activity remains underexplored. This paper investigates the relationship between QoS statistics and user activity using real-world usage logs of an RDS, Thin-Telework System. We analyze time-series data of round-trip time (RTT), the number of sent packets, and the number of received bytes per user. Notably, we find that not only the average RTT but also its temporal fluctuation (e.g., standard deviation over time) and its instantaneous deviation from the mean are significantly associated with user activity. From the users' perspective, these features correspond to QoS unpredictability and the prediction error, respectively, and may provide insights into psychological mechanisms underlying user behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [ESBT: A Scalable and Deterministic Sequence CRDT for Distributed Collaborative Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28101)
*Moulay Driss Mechaoui,Abdessamad Imine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出ESBT标识符分配方案，有效解决序列CRDT的标识符增长和内存消耗问题，显著提升响应速度并减少内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列CRDT虽然保证了强最终一致性，但在长时间、高并发的编辑会话中存在标识符增长失控和内存消耗增加的问题，严重限制了其可扩展性和性能。

Method: 提出扩展Stern-Brocot树（ESBT），一种基于数学原理的标识符分配方案，提供密集、确定性和紧凑的标识符空间，在保持确定性排序和强最终一致性的同时限制标识符增长。

Result: 在50个协作站点、10万次并发操作的实验中：纯插入操作响应速度提升28-88%，混合插入/删除操作提升59-74%；初始和随机插入模式下标识符内存消耗减少50-75%；对抗性中间插入工作负载下响应速度提升86.53%，标识符大小减少92.81%。

Conclusion: ESBT有效解决了现有序列CRDT的主要可扩展性限制，为大规模协同编辑系统提供了高效且可扩展的基础。

Abstract: Modern collaborative editing systems require efficient mechanisms for managing concurrent updates across distributed replicas. Sequence Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) have become the de facto standard for supporting decentralized collaboration; they enable decentralized replicas to apply operations in arbitrary order while converging to a common state. Although existing sequence CRDTs guarantee Strong Eventual Consistency (SEC), they often suffer from uncontrolled identifier growth and increasing memory consumption during long-running, highly concurrent editing sessions, which severely limits their scalability and performance. This paper presents the Extended Stern-Brocot Tree (ESBT), a mathematically grounded identifier allocation scheme for distributed collaborative text editing, providing a dense, deterministic, and compact identifier space. The proposed allocation strategy bounds identifier growth while preserving deterministic ordering and Strong Eventual Consistency. Experimental evaluation using workloads of up to 100,000 concurrent operations generated across 50 collaborating sites shows that ESBT improves responsiveness by 28 to 88% under pure insertions and 59 to 74% under mixed insertion/deletion workloads, while reducing identifier memory consumption by 50 to 75% in beginning and random insertion patterns compared with the best-performing baseline sequence CRDTs (Logoot and LSEQ). Under the adversarial middle-insertion workload (10,000 operations), ESBT further improves responsiveness by 86.53% and reduces identifier size by 92.81%. These results demonstrate that ESBT effectively addresses the principal scalability limitations of existing sequence CRDTs and provides an efficient and scalable foundation for large-scale collaborative editing systems.

</details>


### [249] [SmartGen: Seamless Disaggregated LLM Inference with Selective KV Cache Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28150)
*Xuchuan Luo,Jiacheng Shen,Xin Wang,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartGen，一种选择性KV缓存传输引擎，通过三种数据传输路径（基于性能分析的前瞻传输、并行按需传输和推测传输），解决了分离式LLM推理系统中网络带宽瓶颈问题，将第二个token生成时间(TTST)减少高达4.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM服务系统将推理的预填充和解码阶段分离到不同节点，但在自托管云实例部署中，在节点间传输大量KV缓存会轻易耗尽有限的节点间网络带宽，形成网络瓶颈。

Method: 提出SmartGen系统，通过选择性KV缓存传输缓解网络瓶颈，包含三条数据传输路径：1）基于性能分析的前瞻传输路径，在预填充阶段识别并推送关键KV缓存条目到解码节点；2）并行按需传输路径，在解码阶段同时获取远程和本地KV缓存条目；3）推测传输路径，最终将所有KV缓存传送到解码节点。

Result: SmartGen与典型的全量KV缓存传输方法相比，将第二个token生成时间(TTST)减少高达4.3倍，同时提供相当的后续解码性能和准确性。

Conclusion: 选择性KV缓存传输能够有效缓解分离式LLM推理系统中的网络瓶颈问题，在保持性能和准确性的同时显著降低延迟。

Abstract: Disaggregating the prefill and decoding stages of large language model (LLM) inference into two separate sets of nodes is widely adopted in today's LLM serving systems. However, such an architecture poses significant challenges for self-hosted LLM deployments on rented cloud instances, since transferring enormous key-value (KV) caches between disaggregated nodes can easily saturate the limited inter-node network bandwidth. In this paper, we propose to mitigate the network bottleneck by selectively transferring essential KV cache entries across the two stages. There are two challenges to achieve selective KV cache transfer, i.e., accurate KV selection during the prefill stage, and efficient KV fetching during the decoding stage. To address these challenges, we design SmartGen, a KV cache transfer engine that allows seamless disaggregated LLM inference with three data transfer paths. Specifically, we leverage 1) a profile-based proactive transfer path to identify and push essential KV cache entries to the decoding node during the prefill stage, 2) a parallel on-demand transfer path to simultaneously fetch remote and local KV cache entries during the decoding stage, and 3) a speculative transfer path to finally deliver all KV caches to the decoding node. Experimental results show that SmartGen reduces time-to-second-token by up to 4.3x compared with the typical full KV cache transfer approach while offering comparable subsequent decoding performance and accuracy.

</details>


### [250] [A Cloud Continuum Research Infrastructure for Distributed CPS Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28193)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Giuseppe Tricomi,Luca D'Agati,Andrea Sabbioni,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Giovanni Merlino,Armir Bujari,Paolo Bellavista,Antonio Puliafito*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于云连续体实验的两层参考架构，通过分离研究基础设施层和应用层来支持异构资源组合，并在两个用例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云连续体应用需要能够组合异构的边缘、雾、云和高性能计算资源的实验环境，同时保持分布式部署的可重复性、可观察性和可控性。现有实验环境缺乏对多应用场景的通用支持和对放置、时序和数据溯源等实验因素的系统性考虑。

Method: 提出基于SLICES云连续体蓝图的两层参考架构：研究基础设施层负责暴露和管理分布式资源；应用层按照边缘-雾-云模式组织信息物理工作流，将放置、时序和数据溯源作为一等实验关注点。架构支持边缘层与物理设备交互并执行低延迟感知/安全动作，雾层执行近源协调/中介/流处理逻辑，云层通过分析/优化/可视化整合全局知识。

Result: 通过两个代表性用例验证了该方法：可再生能源社区管理（分布式数字孪生协调和基于时间窗口的能源控制）和AirWatch监控流水线（异常检测、低延迟告警和云端聚合）。在地理分布式基础设施上进行了40次运行的系统性评估活动，比较了虚拟化和物理边缘部署的性能表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的两层架构成功实现了对云连续体应用实验环境的支持，使研究人员能够在同一可编程基础设施基板上部署、定制和比较替代的控制和监控策略，为多领域连续体应用提供了通用的实验平台解决方案。

Abstract: Cloud Continuum applications require experimental environments capable of combining heterogeneous Edge, Fog, Cloud, and high-performance computing resources while preserving reproducibility, observability, and control over distributed deployments. This paper presents a two-level reference architecture for Cloud Continuum experimentation built on top of the SLICES Cloud Continuum Blueprint. The proposed approach separates the research-infrastructure layer, which exposes and manages distributed resources, from the application layer, where Cyber-Physical workflows are organized according to an Edge-Fog-Cloud pattern in which placement, timing, and data provenance are treated as first-class experimental concerns.
  The architecture is designed to support multiple continuum applications rather than a single domain-specific prototype. At the Edge, applications interact with physical devices and perform low-latency sensing or safety actions; at the Fog, they execute near-source coordination, mediation, and stream-processing logic; at the Cloud, they consolidate global knowledge through analytics, optimization, and visualization. This partitioning enables researchers to deploy, customize, and compare alternative control and monitoring strategies over the same programmable infrastructure substrate.
  The approach is validated through two representative use cases: Renewable Energy Community management, where distributed Digital Twin coordination and time-window-based energy control are requested, and AirWatch, a monitoring pipeline focused on anomaly detection, low-latency alerting, and cloud-side aggregation. Both workloads are evaluated through a systematic campaign of 40 runs comparing virtualized and physical edge deployments over a geographically distributed infrastructure.

</details>


### [251] [Queue-Theoretic Admission Control for Multi-Tenant GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28223)
*Sohan Kunkerkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文将GPU集群准入问题形式化为多类别、多资源排队网络，提出“可报价”与“不可行”工作负载的结构分解，为前者给出O(1/(1-ρ))等待时间界，并证明在多资源需求下的最优准入排序是NP难的；在Kueue上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU集群运维人员无法预知待调度工作负载的等待时长，现有系统采用贪婪启发式而无形式化的等待时间保证，缺乏可预测的准入延迟分析。

Method: 将GPU集群准入抽象为多类别、多资源排队网络，证明待处理队列可结构分解为“可报价”与“不可行”工作负载；对可报价工作负载将各集群队列建模为M/G/k系统，有效服务数k由向量装箱缩减确定，并在显式随机占优假设下证明等待时间尺度为O(1/(1-ρ))；通过从向量装箱问题的归约证明最优准入排序为NP难。

Result: 在Kueue（Kubernetes标准工作负载排队系统）上基于CPU、内存和GPU（通过动态资源分配）资源进行验证：向量k_eff正确识别瓶颈资源维度，Little定律精确成立，Erlang-C近似一致地保守高估观测等待时间。

Conclusion: 所提形式化模型与方法能够为GPU集群工作负载等待时间提供理论界限与实际有效预测，支撑更优的准入控制与调度决策。

Abstract: GPU cluster operators cannot predict how long pending workloads will wait for admission. Existing systems use greedy heuristics with no formal wait time guarantees. We formalize GPU cluster admission as a multi-class, multi-resource queueing network and prove a structural decomposition: the pending queue partitions into quotable workloads (bounded wait time under stability) and unfeasible workloads (no finite bound without reconfiguration). For quotable workloads, we model each cluster queue as an M/G/k system where the effective server count k is determined by a vector packing reduction; under an explicit stochastic domination assumption, we establish O(1/(1-rho)) wait time scaling. We prove that optimal admission ordering is NP-hard under multi-dimensional resource demands via reduction from vector bin packing. We validate on Kueue, the standard Kubernetes workload queuing system, using CPU, memory, and GPU (via Dynamic Resource Allocation) resources. The vector k_eff correctly identifies bottleneck resource dimensions, Little's Law holds exactly, and the Erlang-C approximation consistently overestimates observed wait times in the conservative direction.

</details>


### [252] [FAIR-Compute: A Roadmap for Fair and Efficient Allocation of Federated Digital Research Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28290)
*Konstantinos,E. Zachariadis,Ahmed Sayed,Dimitris Fotakis,Angeliki Mathioudaki,Wan Shuen Siaw*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: FAIR-Compute项目研究了联邦数字研究基础设施中的计算资源分配问题，发现现有系统无法衡量资源实际产出价值，简单的调度启发式方法表现接近最优基准，而联邦系统若缺乏管理会产生类似Braess悖论的负面效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算、高通量计算和数据存储需求的增长，稀缺计算资源的分配方式（而不仅仅是资源总量）已成为决定英国研究生产力的关键因素。一旦共享系统需要决定谁在何时、基于什么证据运行任务，其调度器设置就不再纯粹是技术问题，而成为政策问题。

Method: 项目结合了三方面证据：(1) 对英国和国际分配实践（包括EuroHPC、WLCG、ACCESS、JASMIN和DiRAC）的景观综述和利益相关者调研；(2) 基于策略性和不确定性用户报告的分配机制设计模型；(3) 基于公开Fresco/Anvil工作负载轨迹和受控合成压力测试的仿真研究。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1) 分配记录衡量的是占用率（预留资源）而非利用率（完成的有用工作），系统无法回答UKRI和DSIT最关心的问题——资源是否交付生产性价值；(2) 经过调优的简单透明调度启发式方法表现接近离线全信息基准，近期机会在于调优和监测现有调度器而非替换；(3) 联邦系统具有真正价值但若缺乏管理会像非协调路由一样运行，可能最小化平均延迟的同时悄悄集中负载于接收系统，这类似于道路交通经济学中的Braess悖论。

Conclusion: 计算资源分配已从技术问题转变为政策问题，现有系统存在价值衡量缺陷，但通过调优现有调度器可接近最优性能；联邦计算需要协调管理以避免产生负面效应，这项研究为英国研究基础设施的资源分配政策提供了实证基础。

Abstract: As demand for high-performance computing (HPC), high-throughput computing and data storage grows, the way scarce compute is allocated -- not just how much exists -- has become a decisive factor in the productivity of UK research. FAIR-Compute studies allocation in a federated Digital Research Infrastructure (DRI) as a problem at the intersection of algorithmic game theory, transport economics and HPC scheduling. Our central observation is simple: once a shared system must decide who runs, when and under what evidence, its scheduler settings cease to be a purely technical matter and become policy.
  The project combined three strands of evidence: a landscape review of UK and international allocation practice (including EuroHPC, WLCG, ACCESS, JASMIN and DiRAC) supported by a stakeholder survey; a mechanism-design model of allocation under strategic and uncertain user reports; and a simulation study built on the public Fresco/Anvil workload trace and controlled synthetic stress tests. Three results recur across all three strands. First, allocation records measure occupancy (resources reserved) rather than utilisation (useful work done), so the system cannot currently answer the question UKRI and DSIT most want answered -- whether resources deliver productive value. Second, simple, transparent scheduling heuristics perform close to an offline full-information benchmark once tuned, so the near-term opportunity is to tune and instrument existing schedulers rather than replace them. Third, federation is genuinely valuable but behaves like uncoordinated routing when left unmanaged: it can minimise average delay while quietly concentrating load -- and potentially harm -- on the receiving system. This mirrors a classic effect from road-traffic economics (Braess's paradox), where letting everyone independently pick the fastest route can leave the whole network worse off.

</details>


### [253] [A Taxonomy of Performance Metrics for the Distributed Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28407)
*Praveen Kumar Donta,Boris Sedlak,Alfreds Lapkovskis,Alaa Saleh,Ying Li,Victor Casamayor Pujol,Ilir Murturi,Manuel Otero Barbasan,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了分布式计算连续体系统(DCCS)的性能指标分类体系，涵盖计算层、网络层和应用/用户层指标，并引入可持续性、可观测性、适应性等新兴维度，为跨层级DCCS性能评估提供了系统化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能和大规模数据驱动应用的增长，计算需求发生显著变化，应用任务在资源密集型数据中心和资源受限边缘环境间分布。新型计算连续体架构和算法不断涌现，亟需透明一致的性能评估方法。现有评估实践往往聚焦于计算、网络、能效或应用质量等孤立维度，仅提供跨层级DCCS行为的部分视图。

Method: 提出DCCS性能指标的结构化分类体系，将指标组织为计算层、网络层和应用/用户层三类；引入可持续性、可观测性、适应性、数据局部性、迁移感知和连续体碎片化等新兴维度；提供数学公式表述并讨论其在异构动态连续体环境中的相关性；总结指标采集要求（采集范围、采集阶段和测量方法）。

Result: 建立了完整的DCCS性能指标分类体系，明确了各层级指标及其数学表达；阐明了指标采集要求，包括采集范围（单节点、多节点或全系统）和适用场景（运行监控或实验评估）；为异构动态连续体环境中的性能评估提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为DCCS性能评估提供了统一、透明的框架，有助于研究人员和从业者全面理解跨层级系统行为，指导后续性能评估实践和系统优化工作。

Abstract: Performance evaluation is essential for understanding, comparing, and improving computing systems, including Distributed Computing Continuum Systems (DCCS). In recent years, computational requirements have changed substantially with the growth of artificial intelligence and large-scale data-driven applications. These application tasks are increasingly distributed between resource-intensive data centers and resource-constrained edge environments. In this context, novel computing continuum architectures and algorithms are emerging, creating a need for transparent and consistent performance evaluation. However, existing evaluation practices often focus on isolated dimensions, such as computation, networking, energy efficiency, or application-level quality, and therefore provide only a partial view of cross-layer DCCS behavior. This paper presents a structured taxonomy of performance metrics for DCCS. The taxonomy organizes metrics into computing-level, network-level, and application/user-level categories, while also highlighting emerging dimensions such as sustainability, observability, adaptability, data locality, migration awareness, and continuum fragmentation. Further, we provide mathematical formulations and discuss their relevance to heterogeneous and dynamic continuum environments. We also summarize metric acquisition requirements in terms of acquisition scope, acquisition phase, and measurement method. These requirements help clarify whether a metric can be collected from a single node, multiple nodes, or the full system, and whether it is more suitable for operational monitoring or experimental evaluation.

</details>


### [254] [Safe Quotes for Retroactive Liquidity Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28522)
*Peter Bro Miltersen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在跨链恒定乘积流动性池中计算安全报价的一般情况是NP难问题，不存在具有固定近似比的多项式时间算法，但提出了两个实用的输入依赖近似算法来克服这一障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链恒定乘积流动性池在有活跃锁存在时，计算安全报价需要考虑所有虚拟池的最小可能输出。Aanes等人提出了该问题并给出了特殊情况的解法，但一般情况下的计算复杂度是开放问题。本文旨在解决这一开放问题，确定一般情况下的计算复杂度。

Method: 通过计算复杂性理论分析，证明了除非P=NP，否则不存在具有固定乘法近似比的多项式时间算法来计算一般情况下的安全报价。同时设计了两个简单实用的输入依赖近似算法来计算安全报价。

Result: 证明了计算安全报价的一般情况是NP难问题，不存在固定近似比的多项式时间算法。提出了两个输入依赖近似算法，其近似比在实践中可能足够满意，从而绕过了理论障碍。

Conclusion: 虽然理论上一般情况下的安全报价计算具有严重障碍，但通过实用的近似算法可以在实践中有效部署锁定交换功能，平衡了理论复杂性与实际应用需求。

Abstract: Automated market makers exchange assets through liquidity pools whose quoted prices depend on their reserves, with constant product pools being the most common. When such pools reside on different blockchains or shards, a sequence of swaps cannot in general be executed atomically. Aanes et al. introduced lock-swaps and retroactive constant product liquidity pools to provide price guarantees for such a setting. A retroactive pool implicitly maintains a virtual pool for each possible execute/cancel resolution of its active locks. In the presence of active locks, serving a new swap request requires computing a safe quote; a quote with an output that does not exceed the minimum possible output, taken over all virtual pools. The quote being safe is a hard constraint ensuring the integrity of the pool. A soft constraint is to make the quote as close to the minimum possible output as possible. Aanes et al. gave a simple and efficient algorithm for computing the exact minimum when unresolved provides and reclaims of liquidity do not coexist, showed by an explicit example that the algorithm fails in general, and left the computational complexity of the general case open. In this paper, we show that unless P is equal to NP, there is no polynomial time algorithm that computes in the general case a safe quote with any fixed multiplicative approximation ratio (e.g., 50%) relative to the exact minimum. This seems like a severe obstacle for deployment of the lock-swap functionality. However, we also present two simple and practical algorithms for computing safe quotes that have input-dependent approximation ratios that are likely to be satisfactory in practice, thus circumventing that obstacle.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Auditing Emergent LLM-Agent Collaboration through Cooperation-Obligation Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27429)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Hanqing Yang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出iCORE框架，通过统一编码整合交互、任务义务和审计映射，解决LLM-agent系统的可审计性缺口，实现工作健全性和代理分配稳定性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent系统在解决复杂任务时存在可审计性缺口：现有方法虽然记录消息、工具调用等，但无法联合表示剩余工作、责任归属及工作状态转换的证据依据，导致看似合理的输出可能掩盖未完成或未支持的工作及责任分配不当问题。

Method: 提出集成合作-义务表示框架(iCORE)，创建统一编码X=(G,Q,Π)：将可观察交互建模为合作图G，将演化的工作与分配建模为义务图Q，并通过审计映射Π链接二者及可验证属性和证据。该框架作为工作流之上的仪表层，使审计者能够验证工作健全性和代理分配稳定性两个互补属性。

Result: 在两种执行模式下，完整耦合状态能精确重建健全性和分配缺陷。相比被动全状态观察，iCORE-Audit在受控和真实LLM执行中分别实现11.5%和26.4%的轨迹质量绝对提升，以及15.1%和31.0%的终端性能绝对提升。

Conclusion: iCORE成功填补了LLM-agent系统的审计缺口，通过统一表示实现了工作健全性和代理分配稳定性的理论保证，数值实验验证了其有效性，为LLM-agent系统的质量保证提供了可行的审计方案。

Abstract: LLM-agent systems can solve complex tasks through dynamic self-organization and emergent cooperation. Auditing this process is essential because plausible intermediate or final outputs can conceal incomplete or unsupported work and poorly allocated responsibility, ultimately compromising response quality. While existing approaches may record messages, tool calls, provenance, or task dependencies, an auditability gap exists as they do not jointly represent what work remains, who is responsible for it, and what evidence justifies each work-state transition. We address this auditability gap by proposing \emph{Integrated Cooperation-Obligation REpresentation} (iCORE). It creates a unified encoding $X=(G,Q,Π)$ integrating observable interactions as a cooperation graph $G$, evolving work and assignments as an obligation graph $Q$, and the audit map $Π$ linking them with verifiable properties and evidence. This iCORE representation enables the auditor to certify two complementary properties: {Work soundness}, where every active decision-relevant work assertion must have a finite justification through $G$ and $Π$; and {Agent-assignment stability}, which requires that no feasible alternative agent improve the declared contribution value for an evaluated obligation by more than $ε$. We establish local-to-global soundness and assignment-regret guarantees and a performance bound under stated conditions. iCORE is an instrumentation layer over workflows. Numerical results show that the full coupled state exactly reconstructs soundness and assignment defects in two execution modes and that, relative to passive full-state observation, iCORE-Audit yields absolute trajectory-quality improvements of $11.5\%$ and $26.4\%$ in controlled and real-LLM execution, respectively, with corresponding absolute terminal-performance improvements of $15.1\%$ and $31.0\%$.

</details>


### [256] [Stop Shipping AI Agents on Faith: Capability Is Not Production Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27677)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了ProofAgent Index (PAI)，一个AI智能体治理就绪度指数，通过评估、上下文、合规和治理四个维度衡量生产部署准备情况，在医疗和金融领域验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体的发布决策主要依赖能力信号、演示或行为测试，但这些都无法证明智能体是否准备好在生产约束下运行。能力不等于生产就绪度，需要建立一个系统化的治理就绪度评估框架。

Method: 提出ProofAgent Index (PAI)，包含四个维度：评估（测量观察到的行为）、上下文（测量塑造行为的运行环境）、合规（测量与适用规则和控制的对齐程度）、治理（测量组织在运行期间授权、监控、审计和控制智能体的能力）。在开源基础设施ProofAgent Harness中实现该框架。

Result: 在医疗和金融两个高度监管领域进行验证，PAI能够携带保留的就绪信号并区分高风险与低风险配置。上下文工程显著改变可靠性，能力改善行为但不决定就绪度，治理证据必须保持可见而非被平均化处理。

Conclusion: PAI将智能体发布从基于信仰的部署决策转变为可审计的就绪度决策，为AI智能体的生产部署提供了系统化的治理评估方法。

Abstract: AI agents are moving into production workflows where they retrieve information, call tools, maintain state, and act on behalf of users or organizations, but many release decisions still rely on capability signals, demos, or behavioral tests that do not show whether an agent is ready to operate under production constraints. Capability is therefore not production readiness. This paper introduces the ProofAgent Index (PAI), a governance readiness index for AI agents. PAI combines four dimensions of deployment evidence: Evaluation, Context, Compliance, and Governance. Evaluation measures observed behavior, Context measures the operating environment that shapes that behavior, Compliance measures alignment with applicable rules and controls, and Governance measures whether the organization can authorize, monitor, audit, and control the agent during operation. PAI is implemented inside ProofAgent Harness, an open source infrastructure for auditable AI agent evaluation and governance. Validation across two heavily regulated domains, healthcare and finance, shows that PAI carries held out readiness signal and separates higher risk from lower risk configurations. The results show that context engineering strongly changes reliability, capability improves behavior but does not determine readiness, and governance evidence must remain visible rather than averaged away. PAI reframes agent release from a faith based deployment decision into an auditable readiness decision.

</details>


### [257] [Scaling LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Systems: Design Principles and Architectural Scalability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27942)
*Linus Sander,Fengjunjie Pan,Vahid Zolfaghari,Andre Schamschurko,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提炼出四项可扩展多智能体系统设计原则，构建参考架构并进行基准测试。研究发现：当基座LLM超过能力阈值时，规模化可带来约线性成本增长下的准确率提升；性能在中等复杂度达到峰值后因超时等问题下降；一致性问题在各规模层级均构成核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统理论上有望通过协调多个专业化智能体实现集体智能并解决复杂任务，但架构设计空间尚缺乏系统化整理与广泛认可的设计原则，且系统的可扩展性特征仅被部分理解，亟需实证指导。

Method: 通过结构化分析先前工作提炼四项设计原则（简洁性、弹性反馈、含可选环的顺序工作流、基于摘要的通信），将其具象化为以受限有向工作流图形式化描述的参考架构；在标准化终端系统工程任务基准上，使用两个能力不同的LLM评估四种复杂度递增的架构配置。

Result: 1）规模化在基座LLM超过最低能力阈值时带来可量化的准确率提升，成本呈约线性增长；2）性能在中等复杂度达到峰值，随后因超时与评估限制而下降；3）一致性问题在各规模层级均为核心挑战。

Conclusion: 为实践者提供了具体的设计指导，并指出一致性与评估标准化是未来研究的关键方向；强调了在规模化架构中平衡复杂度与稳定性、以及提升智能体间一致性的必要性。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have the potential to enable collective intelligence and scale toward solving highly complex tasks through coordinated ensembles of specialized agents. However, despite their theoretical potential, the architectural design space remains largely non-systematized and lacks broadly established design principles. Furthermore, the scalability characteristics of such systems are only partially understood so far. This paper makes two contributions. We first distill four design principles for scalable MAS architectures from a structured analysis of prior work: simplicity, elastic feedback, sequential workflows with optional loops, and summary-based communication. We operationalize these principles in a reference architecture whose topology is formalized as a constrained directed workflow graph, and we evaluate four configurations of increasing complexity on a standardized benchmark of terminal-based system engineering tasks using two LLMs of differing capability. Our findings show that scaling yields measurable accuracy improvements with approximately linear cost growth, but only when the underlying LLM exceeds a minimum capability threshold. Performance peaks at intermediate complexity, then degrades due to timeouts and evaluation limitations. In addition, persistent consistency issues emerge as a central challenge across all scaling levels. These results provide concrete design guidance for practitioners and highlight consistency and evaluation standardization as key targets for future research.

</details>


### [258] [Argonaut: Interactive Visual Exploration for Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27946)
*Srijoni Majumdar,Chuhao Qin,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Argonaut，一个轻量级、容器化的优化仪表板，首次实现了对分布式离散选择优化问题的交互式、可视化全流程探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式离散选择优化在去中心化环境中难以探索和理解，现有方法多为集中式，只能可视化最终结果，优化过程如同黑箱，缺乏对多智能体决策过程和全局目标形成的可观测性。

Method: 开发了Argonaut系统，这是一个基于Node.js界面、具有可扩展Java和Python优化后端的轻量级容器化优化仪表板。用户可以上传数据集、构建智能体和选项、实时修改决策空间和参数，运行多种算法后端来观察每个配置如何影响局部智能体决策和全局目标。

Result: 在真实世界的家庭用电、共享出行和传感器数据交换数据集上进行了评估，系统可扩展至5600个智能体和100万个解决方案，典型运行时间（200个智能体、100个决策属性）在30秒内完成。

Conclusion: Argonaut将系统构建、优化和分析统一到一个交互式循环中，首次实现了人机协作的分布式离散选择优化过程，使其从单次黑箱计算转变为可交互、可理解的探索过程。

Abstract: Distributed discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings is often hard to explore and navigate: disentangling what other agents choose, how their choices are interdependent, and how they collectively reach a global objective quickly becomes intractable as the system scales. The major limitation is observability of the search process. Existing methods are largely centralized and offer limited support, visualizing only the final solution or providing algorithm backends over a fixed dataset, so how a solution is reached stays a black box. We present Argonaut, a lightweight, containerized optimization dashboard that enables interactive, visual exploration of the entire search process for multi-agent discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings. Users upload datasets, construct agents and options, modify the decision space and its parameters on the fly, and run multiple algorithm backends to inspect how each configuration shapes local agent decisions and the resulting global objective. By uniting system construction, optimization, and analysis in one interactive loop, the first of its kind, Argonaut makes distributed discrete-choice optimization a human-in-the-loop process rather than a one-shot, black-box computation. We evaluate Argonaut on real-world household-electricity, shared-mobility, and sensor-data-exchange datasets scaling to 5600 agents and up to 1M solutions under brute force. Built on a Node.js interface with extensible Java and Python optimization backends, it maintains a typical runtime of 200 agents over 100 decision attributes in under 30 seconds.

</details>


### [259] [$Σ$-Mem: An Online Reliability Memory for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27958)
*Peilin Feng,Suorong Yang,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Σ-Mem，一种在线可靠性记忆系统，用于记录多智能体系统中各智能体的历史能力证据和关系证据，实现了自适应协调并显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的记忆系统主要保存交互内容，缺乏对智能体可靠性及其适用条件的建模。在多智能体系统中，中央模型难以直接验证其他智能体的响应，因此需要一种能够记录和更新智能体可靠性信息的记忆机制。

Method: 引入Σ-Mem在线可靠性记忆系统，将历史能力证据（针对单个智能体）和智能体关系证据（跨智能体集合）维护为实对称状态矩阵，通过事后正确性反馈进行在线更新。利用Weyl不等式保证每次事件级更新引起的谱变化有界，实现无需重训练底层模型的稳定在线适应。提供统一的读写接口，支持残差引导、响应无关路由和可靠性加权投票等多种应用。

Result: 在五个Qwen系列模型上的实验表明：Σ-Mem能够适应反事实可靠性变化，泛化到未见过的智能体和新任务领域；直接记忆读取在完整OOD评估集上优于多数投票和最佳固定智能体；随着正确性反馈增加，性能持续提升，证明系统逐步积累可操作的可靠性信息。

Conclusion: 可靠性记忆可作为LLM多智能体系统自适应协调的可复用基础，为解决智能体信任建模问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Memory is central to long-horizon LLM agents, yet existing memory systems primarily preserve interaction content rather than modeling which agents can be trusted and under what conditions. This limitation is particularly important in multi-agent systems, where a central model may be unable to directly verify plausible or correlated peer responses. We introduce $Σ$-Mem, an online reliability memory that records historical competence evidence for individual peers and peer relationship evidence across the peer set. Both forms of evidence are maintained as real symmetric states and updated from post-decision correctness feedback. By Weyl's inequality, the spectral change caused by each event-level update is bounded, enabling stable online adaptation without retraining the underlying models. $Σ$-Mem provides a general write-and-read interface: the same memory can be used for residual steering of a central model, response-free peer routing, or reliability-weighted voting. Across five Qwen-family models, $Σ$-Mem adapts to counterfactual reliability shifts and generalizes to unseen peers and task domains. Direct memory readouts also outperform majority voting and the best fixed peer over the full OOD evaluation set. Moreover, performance improves consistently as more correctness feedback becomes available, indicating that $Σ$-Mem progressively accumulates actionable reliability information. These results establish reliability memory as a reusable foundation for adaptive coordination in LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [260] [VISA: A Structured Description Protocol for Agent-Based Simulation Models Towards Machine Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28027)
*Zhou He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了VISA协议，通过8个结构化表格和19条可执行一致性规则解决ABM模型复现难题，并提供了LLA驱动的完整工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于主体的模型（ABM）难以复现：模型行为分散在散文叙述、平台特定代码和隐式假设中，导致两个读者经常从同一文档重构出不同模型。

Method: 提出了VISA——一种基于符号的结构化描述协议，包含8个互联表格（代理层4个：Agent/Variable/Sensing/Internal Function，模型层4个：Associated Data/Input/Output/Schedule/Validation），遵循最小完备性原则。提供19条可执行一致性规则将模型有效性转化为可检查属性，以及3个可复用的LLM可执行技能（撰写、检查、代码生成）。

Result: 在三个独立作者的外部ABM（跨三个平台）上验证：直接从VISA规范复现了两个跨语言模型（NetLogo到Python）；成功将第三个工业AnyLogic模型捕获为8个表格（通过全部19条规则），诚实划定专有移动库和不可用数据阻碍复现的位置。

Conclusion: VISA将复现障碍从模型本身（不可见）转移到命名的、局部化的依赖项（可操作），为ABM模型的可复现性提供了系统性解决方案。

Abstract: Agent-based models (ABMs) are difficult to reproduce: their behavior is spread across prose narratives, platform-specific code, and implicit assumptions, so that two readers routinely reconstruct different models from the same documentation. We present VISA, a structured, symbol-based description protocol that specifies a model in eight interconnected tables---four at the agent level (Agent, Variable, Sensing, Internal Function) and four at the model level (Associated Data, Input/Output, Schedule, Validation)---under the principle of minimality with completeness. VISA makes a model machine-parseable and unambiguous via two artifacts: nineteen executable consistency rules that turn model validity into a checkable property, and three reusable LLM-executable skills (authoring, checking, and code generation) that operationalize the full author--check--code--reproduce loop. We validate the protocol on three external, independently authored ABMs spanning three platforms: we reproduce two cross-language (NetLogo to Python) directly from their VISA specifications, and we capture a third, an industrial AnyLogic model, in eight tables (passing all nineteen rules) while honestly demarcating where reproduction is blocked by a proprietary movement library and unavailable data---itself a transparency contribution. VISA moves the reproduction barrier from the model, where it is invisible, to a named, localized dependency, where it is actionable.

</details>


### [261] [AgentRadio: Passive Awareness for Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28430)
*Xinxing Ren,Qianbo Zang,Ziyan Wang,Caelum Forder,Suman Deb,Peter Carroll,Zekun Guo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出AgentRadio，一种异步消息传递层，使多智能体系统在长周期代码理解任务中实现实时协作，将任务解决率从32.3%提升至62.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型代码库理解是LLM智能体的长周期任务，单一智能体解决率仅为32.3%。现有异步多智能体系统仅在阶段边界进行信息交换，无法在执行过程中及时共享发现，而代码理解的子任务相互依赖，需要智能体在执行过程中协调。

Method: 提出AgentRadio异步消息传递层，为编码智能体提供三个原语：线程、消息和等待提及。等待提及作为后台任务运行，在不中断前台工作的情况下展示队友消息，使智能体在执行过程中保持被动感知并融入新发现。采用五阶段协议（分工与协商）组织四个智能体协作。

Result: 四个AgentRadio组织的智能体解决62.1%任务，比单一智能体高29.8个百分点，超过配备更新Opus 4.8的Claude Code（57.2%）。评分分析显示收益随任务难度增加而增长，验证了中途修正机制的有效性。

Conclusion: AgentRadio通过异步消息传递实现智能体执行期间的实时协作，有效解决了长周期代码理解任务中的智能体协调问题，显著提升了任务解决率，代码已开源。

Abstract: Understanding large codebases is a long-horizon task for Large Language Model (LLM) agents: answering a single question can require building and running the software, tracing execution across files, and synthesizing evidence over tens of minutes. On SWE-Atlas QnA, a benchmark of long-horizon questions over production repositories, a single Claude Code agent (Opus 4.6) resolves only 32.3% of tasks. Dividing the work among agents with clean contexts mitigates this limitation. However, the subtasks of code comprehension are interdependent. One agent's findings can rewrite another's task, so agents must coordinate during execution, not only at phase boundaries. Existing multi-agent systems support such exchange only between phases, through staged handoffs or synchronized rounds. Communication and work remain mutually exclusive. A discovery made mid-execution cannot be shared until the next boundary. We present AgentRadio, an asynchronous message-passing layer that equips coding-agent harnesses with three primitives: threads, messages, and waiting for mentions. The last runs as a background task, surfacing teammates' messages without interrupting foreground work, so each agent remains passively aware of its peers and folds new findings into its ongoing task. Under a five-phase protocol of division of labor and negotiation, four agents organized by AgentRadio resolve 62.1% of tasks, 29.8 points above a single agent and above Claude Code with the newer Opus 4.8 (57.2%). Rubric-level analysis shows the gain growing with task difficulty, consistent with mid-course correction as the underlying mechanism. Our code is available at https://github.com/Coral-Protocol/AgentRadio.

</details>


### [262] [Using Theory of Mind to Arbitrate between Social and Non-social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28601)
*Lance Ying,Ryan Truong,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Samuel J. Gershman*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了理性心理化模型，通过心理理论（Theory of Mind）推断他人行为的信息价值，量化解释人类如何在社交学习与非社交学习之间进行权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交学习是一种强大的学习机制，但有时人类会选择直接经验而非社交学习，即使这需要付出时间和认知资源成本。目前尚不清楚人类如何平衡社交学习与非社交学习策略。

Method: 提出理性心理化（Rational Mentalizing）模型，该模型通过推理他人目标及其未来行动的信息性来估计社交学习的效用，并将其与非社交学习的效用进行权衡比较。研究设计了一个新颖的游戏任务，让玩家在观察其他代理或探索环境之间做出选择，以此验证模型。

Result: 理性心理化模型能够定量地捕捉人类在社交学习与非社交学习策略之间的权衡决策，模型预测与人类实际行为相符。

Conclusion: 选择性社交学习由心理理论引导，服务于效用最大化，这揭示了人类如何灵活选择学习策略的认知机制。

Abstract: Social learning is a powerful mechanism through which agents learn about the world from others. However, humans sometimes choose direct experience over social learning, which can carry time and cognitive resource costs. How do people balance social and non-social learning? We propose a Rational Mentalizing model of the decision to engage in social learning. This model estimates the utility of social learning by reasoning about another agent's goal and the informativeness of their future actions. It then weighs the utility of social learning against the utility of non-social learning. Using a novel game where players choose between observing other agents or exploring the environment, we show that the Rational Mentalizing model can quantitatively capture human trade-offs between these strategies. These findings suggest that selective social learning is guided by 'Theory of Mind' in the service of utility maximization.

</details>
